- **pstack-principle-attack-the-premise**: Apply when two or more fixes that share one premise have failed the same gate. ([alandotcom/pi-config](https://github.com/alandotcom/pi-config/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack/commands/pstack-principle-attack-the-premise))

- **pstack-principle-boundary-discipline**: Apply when wiring validation, error handling, or framework adapters. ([alandotcom/pi-config](https://github.com/alandotcom/pi-config/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack/commands/pstack-principle-boundary-discipline))

- **pstack-principle-build-the-lever**: Apply to any non-trivial work, not just bulk work: edits, migrations, analyses, checks. - **pstack-principle-encode-lessons-in-structure**: Apply when you catch yourself writing the same instruction a second time, or notice a recurring correction. ([alandotcom/pi-config](https://github.com/alandotcom/pi-config/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack/commands/pstack-principle-encode-lessons-in-structure))

- **pstack-principle-explain-the-number**: Apply before you trust, report, or act on a number you measured: a speedup, a regression, a throughput, a latency, or an eval result. ([alandotcom/pi-config](https://github.com/alandotcom/pi-config/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack/commands/pstack-principle-explain-the-number))
- **pstack-principle-fix-root-causes**: Apply when debugging. ([alandotcom/pi-config](https://github.com/alandotcom/pi-config/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack/commands/pstack-principle-fix-root-causes))

- **pstack-principle-foundational-thinking**: Apply before writing logic: choosing core types and data structures, sequencing scaffold-vs-feature work, asking what concurrent actors share. - **pstack-principle-foundational-thinking**: On request, apply the foundational thinking principle to the requested task. ([lhotwll217/skills](https://github.com/lhotwll217/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pstack/skills/pstack-principle-foundational-thinking))
- **pstack-principle-guard-the-context-window**: Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. ([alandotcom/pi-config](https://github.com/alandotcom/pi-config/tree/HEAD/skills/pstack/commands/pstack-principle-guard-the-context-window))
- **pstack-principle-guard-the-context-window**: On request, apply the guard the context window principle to the requested task. ([lhotwll217/skills](https://github.com/lhotwll217/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pstack/skills/pstack-principle-guard-the-context-window))
- **pstack-principle-laziness-protocol**: Apply when refactoring, evaluating diff size, or tempted to add abstractions, layers, or signal threading. - **pstack-principle-laziness-protocol**: On request, apply the laziness protocol principle to the requested task. ([lhotwll217/skills](https://github.com/lhotwll217/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pstack/skills/pstack-principle-laziness-protocol))
- **pstack-principle-make-operations-idempotent**: Apply when designing commands, lifecycle steps, or processing loops that run amid crashes, restarts, and retries. - **pstack-principle-make-operations-idempotent**: On request, apply the make operations idempotent principle to the requested task. ([lhotwll217/skills](https://github.com/lhotwll217/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pstack/skills/pstack-principle-make-operations-idempotent))
- **pstack-principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis**: Apply when introducing a new internal API while old callers still exist. - **pstack-principle-never-block-on-the-human**: Apply when tempted to ask 'should I do X?' on reversible work. - **pstack-principle-never-block-on-the-human**: On request, apply the never block on the human principle to the requested task. ([lhotwll217/skills](https://github.com/lhotwll217/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/pstack/skills/pstack-principle-never-block-on-the-human))

- **pstack-principle-prove-it-works**: Apply after completing a task, before declaring done. - **pstack-principle-redesign-from-first-principles**: Apply when integrating a new requirement into an existing design. - **pstack-principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state**: Apply when concurrent actors might write to the same file, branch, key, or state object. - **pstack-principle-sequence-verifiable-units**: Apply to multi-step work (sweeps, migrations, runs of similar edits) and to how you stack commits and PRs. - **pstack-principle-subtract-before-you-add**: Apply when sequencing an addition, refactor, or rewrite. - **pstack-principle-test-behavior-not-implementation**: Apply when you write, change, or keep a test. - **pstack-principle-type-system-discipline**: Apply when designing types, reviewing a function signature, or writing code in any statically-typed language. - **psych-development-personality-social-clinical-and-neurodiversity**: Use when the question concerns individuals and groups: development across the lifespan, personality and the trait models and their predictive validity, motivation, social psychology including the classic studies and which of them survived scrutiny, clinical psychology and what the outcome evidence says actually works, intelligence and what the measures do and do not capture, and neurodiversity. Reports the evidence and its quality; not clinical advice or a basis for diagnosis. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences/skills/psych-development-personality-social-clinical-and-neurodiversity) · ★2)
- **psych-developmental**: Developmental psychology validation for game development. Invoked by game-psychologist agent at validation checkpoints. ([Hexcelerate/web-game-dev](https://github.com/Hexcelerate/web-game-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/psych-developmental))
- **psych-epistemic-situation-and-replication-reform**: Use before relying on any finding from these fields: what the replication crisis actually established and the genuinely encouraging finding alongside it, the reliability gradient as the practical takeaway, how to read a claim in these fields, why the fields are methodologically hard, and what the reform movement changed in practice. Includes the router for the whole psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences reference. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences/skills/psych-epistemic-situation-and-replication-reform) · ★2)
- **psych-learning**: Cognitive and learning science validation for game development. Invoked by game-psychologist agent at validation checkpoints. ([Hexcelerate/web-game-dev](https://github.com/Hexcelerate/web-game-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/psych-learning))
- **psych-motivation**: Motivation and engagement validation for game development. Invoked by game-psychologist agent at validation checkpoints. ([Hexcelerate/web-game-dev](https://github.com/Hexcelerate/web-game-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/psych-motivation))
- **psych-perception-memory-learning-cognition-and-emotion**: Use when working with the well-established core of cognitive psychology: perception and attention including change blindness and attentional limits, memory and its systems, reconstruction and the reliability of eyewitness recall, learning and conditioning, cognition, reasoning and the biases that replicated versus those that did not, and emotion and the competing theories of what an emotion is. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences/skills/psych-perception-memory-learning-cognition-and-emotion) · ★2)
- **psych-reference**: Use when checking what is live (where replication reform actually stands, and the social-media-and-adolescent-mental-health dispute presented as a genuine dispute rather than resolved, verified August 2026), correcting a behavioural-science misconception, finding the canon, or needing a picker and the red flags to check in any behavioural-science claim. Companion to the other psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences skills. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences/skills/psych-reference) · ★2)
- **psych-review**: Multi-perspective code review through simulated cognitive disorder lenses. Dispatches parallel reviewer agents, each with a distinct psychological bias, and aggregates consensus findings. Use when the user asks for "psych review", "multi-perspective review", "paranoid review", "review from different angles", or names a specific disorder persona. Also triggers on "psych-review --all", "psych-review --pack security", etc. &lt;example&gt;User: "psych review this branch"&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;User: "run a paranoid review of the PR"&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;User: "psych-review --pack security"&lt;/example&gt; ([Lanqu/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/Lanqu/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/psych-review/skills/psych-review))
- **psych-sociology-institutions-culture-and-the-weird-problem**: Use when the unit of analysis is social rather than individual: sociological thinking and what it adds, the theory traditions and their disagreements, stratification and social mobility, network structure and its consequences, institutions and organizations, deviance and social control, religion and the secularization debate, culture and anthropology and the ethnographic method, and the WEIRD problem — why a discipline built on unrepresentative samples generalizes badly. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/psychology-sociology-cultural-sciences/skills/psych-sociology-institutions-culture-and-the-weird-problem) · ★2)
- **psychbull-inclusion-and-coding**: Use when defining eligibility criteria and coding studies for a Psychological Bulletin review or meta-analysis — inclusion/exclusion rules, a codebook, double-coding, and inter-rater reliability. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Bulletin-Skills/skills/psychbull-inclusion-and-coding) · ★1.1k)
- **psychbull-literature-search-strategy**: Use when designing and documenting the systematic literature search for a Psychological Bulletin review or meta-analysis — databases, search strings, grey literature, deduplication, and a PRISMA-compliant records flow. Builds a reproducible search; it does not screen or code studies. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Bulletin-Skills/skills/psychbull-literature-search-strategy) · ★1.1k)
- **psychbull-meta-analysis-methods**: Use when computing effect sizes and fitting the meta-analytic model for a Psychological Bulletin manuscript — effect-size metrics, random-effects vs. fixed-effect choice, dependent effect sizes (RVE / multilevel), and heterogeneity (Q, I², τ², prediction interval). Guides the core synthesis; moderators and bias diagnostics live in psychbull-moderators-and-bias. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Bulletin-Skills/skills/psychbull-meta-analysis-methods) · ★1.1k)
- **psychbull-moderators-and-bias**: Use when explaining heterogeneity and probing robustness in a Psychological Bulletin meta-analysis — moderator/subgroup analysis, meta-regression, and publication-bias diagnostics (funnel, Egger, trim-and-fill, PET-PEESE, p-curve, selection models) plus sensitivity analyses. Extends the core model; estimation lives in psychbull-meta-analysis-methods. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Bulletin-Skills/skills/psychbull-moderators-and-bias) · ★1.1k)
- **psychbull-open-science-and-transparency**: Use when planning and assembling the transparency package for a Psychological Bulletin submission — protocol preregistration (PROSPERO/OSF), TOP-compliant disclosure, and depositing the database, codebook, and analysis scripts. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Bulletin-Skills/skills/psychbull-open-science-and-transparency) · ★1.1k)
- **psychbull-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Psychological Bulletin revise-and-resubmit. - **psychrev-argument-development**: Use when deriving predictions from a Psychological Review theory and confronting them with existing data and rival models — the journal's substitute for an empirical results section. - **psychrev-boundary-conditions**: Use when setting the scope of a Psychological Review theory — what it explains, what it does NOT, and (for formal models) whether its parameters are identifiable. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Review-Skills/skills/psychrev-boundary-conditions) · ★1.1k)
- **psychrev-conceptual-exhibits**: Use when designing the figures of a Psychological Review manuscript — model/architecture diagrams and simulation-of-model-behavior plots (NOT experiments). - **psychrev-contribution-framing**: Use when articulating the theoretical advance of a Psychological Review manuscript — what the field can explain after the paper that it could not before, stated against the specific prior models it improves on. - **psychrev-literature-positioning**: Use when locating a Psychological Review manuscript within its theoretical conversation — naming the theories it extends and, above all, the rival models it must beat or subsume. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Review-Skills/skills/psychrev-literature-positioning) · ★1.1k)
- **psychrev-rebuttal**: Use when a Psychological Review decision letter (usually a major revision) has arrived and you must write the response document and revise the theory. - **psychrev-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for a Psychological Review manuscript — Editorial Manager portal, APA format and length, abstract, masking, references, ethics, and (for computational models) sharing the author's own model code. - **psychrev-theory-construction**: Use when building the actual theory or formal/computational model for a Psychological Review manuscript — turning a framed problem into explicit assumptions, mechanisms, formal structure, and derivations. - **psychrev-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether an idea is a genuine theoretical problem with the scope and generality Psychological Review demands — and, critically, whether it is theory rather than an empirical report. - **psychrev-workflow**: Use when deciding which psychrev-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing a theory or formal-model manuscript from problem framing through masked-review revision for a Psychological Review submission. Use whenever preparing, reviewing, or explaining a Portuguese IRS / Modelo 3 declaration, selecting anexos, or reasoning about Portuguese personal income tax.
